Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the bonding structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 390. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 432 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_390(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 390. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^432(S, F) is trivial. QED.
